1. using System;
2. using Avalonia.Controls;
3. using Avalonia.Layout;
4. 
5. namespace VideoEmpty.UI;
6. 
7. public sealed class SettingsDialog : Window
8. {
9.     private readonly CheckBox _enableDelete;
10.     private readonly TextBox _daysBox;
11. 
12.     public bool? EnableAutoDelete { get; private set; }
13.     public int? AutoDeleteDays { get; private set; }
14. 
15.     public SettingsDialog(UiSettings settings)
16.     {
17.         Title = "Settings";
18.         Width = 420;
19.         Height = 220;
20. 
21.         _enableDelete = new CheckBox
22.         {
23.             Content = "Auto-delete backup files after N days",
24.             IsChecked = settings.AutoDeleteBackupsEnabled
25.         };
26.         _daysBox = new TextBox { Text = settings.AutoDeleteBackupsDays.ToString(), Width = 80 };
27. 
28.         var ok = new Button { Content = "Save", IsDefault = true };
29.         var cancel = new Button { Content = "Cancel", IsCancel = true };
30. 
31.         ok.Click += (_, _) =>
32.         {
33.             EnableAutoDelete = _enableDelete.IsChecked == true;
34.             AutoDeleteDays = int.TryParse(_daysBox.Text, out var d) ? Math.Max(1, d) : 90;
35.             Close(true);
36.         };
37.         cancel.Click += (_, _) => Close(false);
38. 
39.         Content = new StackPanel
40.         {
41.             Margin = new Avalonia.Thickness(12),
42.             Spacing = 10,
43.             Children =
44.             {
45.                 _enableDelete,
46.                 new StackPanel
47.                 {
48.                     Orientation = Orientation.Horizontal,
49.                     Spacing = 8,
50.                     Children = { new TextBlock { Text = "Days:" }, _daysBox }
51.                 },
52.                 new StackPanel
53.                 {
54.                     Orientation = Orientation.Horizontal,
55.                     HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Right,
56.                     Spacing = 8,
57.                     Children = { ok, cancel }
58.                 }
59.             }
60.         };
61.     }
62. }
63.

1. using System;
2. using System.Collections.Generic;
3. using System.IO;
4. using System.Linq;
5. using System.Security.Cryptography;
6. using System.Text;
7. using System.Text.Json;
8. 
9. namespace VideoEmpty.UI;
10. 
11. public sealed class UiSettings
12. {
13.     public List<string> RecentProjects { get; set; } = new();
14.     public bool AutoDeleteBackupsEnabled { get; set; }
15.     public int AutoDeleteBackupsDays { get; set; } = 90;
16. }
17. 
18. public static class UiSettingsStore
19. {
20.     private static readonly JsonSerializerOptions JsonOptions = new() { WriteIndented = true };
21. 
22.     public static string AppDataDir
23.     {
24.         get
25.         {
26.             var baseDir = Environment.GetFolderPath(Environment.SpecialFolder.LocalApplicationData);
27.             var dir = Path.Combine(baseDir, "VideoEmpty");
28.             Directory.CreateDirectory(dir);
29.             return dir;
30.         }
31.     }
32. 
33.     public static string SettingsPath => Path.Combine(AppDataDir, "ui-settings.json");
34. 
35.     public static UiSettings Load()
36.     {
37.         try
38.         {
39.             if (!File.Exists(SettingsPath)) return new UiSettings();
40.             return JsonSerializer.Deserialize<UiSettings>(File.ReadAllText(SettingsPath), JsonOptions) ?? new UiSettings();
41.         }
42.         catch
43.         {
44.             return new UiSettings();
45.         }
46.     }
47. 
48.     public static void Save(UiSettings settings)
49.     {
50.         File.WriteAllText(SettingsPath, JsonSerializer.Serialize(settings, JsonOptions));
51.     }
52. 
53.     public static string EnsureProjectRoot()
54.     {
55.         var dir = Path.Combine(AppDataDir, "projects");
56.         Directory.CreateDirectory(dir);
57.         return dir;
58.     }
59. 
60.     public static string GetProjectPath(string projectName)
61.     {
62.         var safe = string.Join("_", projectName.Split(Path.GetInvalidFileNameChars(), StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries)).Trim();
63.         if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(safe)) safe = "project";
64.         return Path.Combine(EnsureProjectRoot(), $"{safe}.veproj");
65.     }
66. 
67.     public static string GetBackupDir(string projectPath)
68.     {
69.         var hash = Convert.ToHexString(SHA256.HashData(Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(projectPath))).Substring(0, 10);
70.         var safe = Path.GetFileNameWithoutExtension(projectPath);
71.         var dir = Path.Combine(AppDataDir, "backups", $"{safe}_{hash}");
72.         Directory.CreateDirectory(dir);
73.         return dir;
74.     }
75. }
76.
```

</details>

<sub>⏱️ 3m 28s</sub>

### ✅ `view`

**view the file at D:\Source\Repos\VideoEmpty\README.md.**

D:\Source\Repos\VideoEmpty\README.md

<details>
<summary>71 lines</summary>

````
1. # VideoEmpty
2. 
3. Cross-platform desktop app to add reusable, well-aligned caption "templates" to screen recordings — built with C# / .NET 8, Avalonia UI, SkiaSharp, and FFmpeg.
4. 
5. > **Status:** scaffold + working in-process API + Avalonia UI + HTTP server + MCP server + Skia template renderer + FFmpeg export pipeline.
6. 
7. ## Why
8. 
9. CapCut has no template grouping, forcing copy/paste and manual alignment of repeated caption groups. VideoEmpty lets you define a caption *once* (composed of shapes, borders, text, animation, sound effects), then click on the video preview to place instances at the playhead — every instance stays consistent.
10. 
11. ## Solution layout
12. 
13. ```
14. src/
15.   VideoEmpty.Core/        # Domain model, IVideoEmptyApi, .veproj JSON, built-in templates
16.   VideoEmpty.Rendering/   # SkiaSharp template rasterizer + FFmpeg/ffprobe wrappers + exporter
17.   VideoEmpty.UI/          # Avalonia desktop app (Windows + macOS)
18.   VideoEmpty.Server/      # ASP.NET Core HTTP wrapper around IVideoEmptyApi
19.   VideoEmpty.Mcp/         # JSON-RPC stdio MCP server exposing IVideoEmptyApi tools
20. tests/
21.   VideoEmpty.Core.Tests/
22.   VideoEmpty.Rendering.Tests/
23. ```
24. 
25. `IVideoEmptyApi` is the single API surface; UI calls it directly, HTTP and MCP are thin adapters — guaranteeing parity across local, remote, and AI-driven use.
26. 
27. ## Built-in templates
28. 
29. - **Step** — white rectangle, black border, black centered text. Two rows: step number + title. Default slide-in/out from left.
30. - **Comment** — black rectangle, white border, white text aligned to bottom. Two rows. Default slide-in/out from left.
31. 
32. Both can be customized or duplicated as starting points.
33. 
34. ## Requirements
35. 
36. - .NET 8 SDK
37. - FFmpeg + ffprobe — **the app installs these for you on first run** (Windows: `winget install Gyan.FFmpeg`; macOS: `brew install ffmpeg`). You can also install manually and place them on `PATH`, or set `VIDEOEMPTY_FFMPEG` / `VIDEOEMPTY_FFPROBE`.
38. - Logs are written to `%LOCALAPPDATA%\VideoEmpty\logs\videoempty.log` (Windows) or `~/.local/state/VideoEmpty/logs/videoempty.log` (macOS/Linux). Toolbar buttons: **Install FFmpeg…** and **Open Log**.
39. 
40. ## Build & test
41. 
42. ```powershell
43. dotnet build
44. dotnet test
45. ```
46. 
47. ## Run
48. 
49. ```powershell
50. # Desktop UI
51. dotnet run --project src/VideoEmpty.UI
52. 
53. # HTTP server (ASP.NET Core, default http://localhost:5000)
54. dotnet run --project src/VideoEmpty.Server
55. 
56. # MCP server (stdio, JSON-RPC framed with Content-Length headers)
57. dotnet run --project src/VideoEmpty.Mcp
58. ```
59. 
60. ## Project file format
61. 
62. `.veproj` — pretty-printed JSON; round-trips through `ProjectJson`. Element types use a `kind` discriminator (`shape` / `text`).
63. 
64. ## How placement works
65. 
66. 1. Pause / scrub to the desired time.
67. 2. Click a template in the left palette (it becomes "armed").
68. 3. Click on the video preview → text dialog appears (multi-line; lines map to text elements top-to-bottom).
69. 4. The instance is added with its **center** at the click point and the template's default duration.
70. 5. Edit start/duration/position/text in the right panel; export to MP4 when done.
71.
````

</details>

292.     private int FrameDurationMs()
293.     {
294.         var fps = _project.VideoFps > 0 ? _project.VideoFps : 30.0;
295.         return Math.Max(1, (int)Math.Round(1000.0 / fps));
296.     }

298.     private void TogglePlay()
299.     {
300.         if (_project.VideoDurationMs <= 0) return;
301.         if (_playTimer is { IsEnabled: true })
302.         {
303.             _playTimer.Stop();
304.             PlayPauseButton.Content = "▶ Play";
305.             return;
306.         }
307.         _playTimer ??= new DispatcherTimer();
308.         _playTimer.Interval = TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(FrameDurationMs());
309.         _playTimer.Tick -= OnPlayTick;
310.         _playTimer.Tick += OnPlayTick;
311.         _playTimer.Start();
312.         PlayPauseButton.Content = "⏸ Pause";
313.     }

315.     private void OnPlayTick(object? sender, EventArgs e)
316.     {
317.         var next = _currentTimeMs + FrameDurationMs();
318.         if (next >= _project.VideoDurationMs)
319.         {
320.             if (LoopCheck.IsChecked == true) next = 0;
321.             else { _playTimer?.Stop(); PlayPauseButton.Content = "▶ Play"; next = _project.VideoDurationMs; }
322.         }
323.         TimeSlider.Value = next;
324.     }

332.     private static string FormatTime(int ms)
333.     {
334.         var ts = TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(Math.Max(0, ms));
335.         return $"{(int)ts.TotalMinutes}:{ts.Seconds:00}.{ts.Milliseconds:000}";
336.     }

338.     private static bool IsHorizontalSlide(AnimationStyle style) =>
339.         style is AnimationStyle.SlideLeft or AnimationStyle.SlideRight;
340. 
341.     private static Animation CloneAnimation(Animation source) => new()
342.     {
343.         Enter = source.Enter,
344.         Exit = source.Exit,
345.         EnterMs = source.EnterMs,
346.         ExitMs = source.ExitMs
347.     };
348. 
349.     private (double centerX, double centerY, Animation? animationOverride) ResolveClickPlacement(
350.         Template template, double clickX, double clickY)
351.     {
352.         var anim = template.Animation;
353.         bool horizontalTemplate = IsHorizontalSlide(anim.Enter) || IsHorizontalSlide(anim.Exit);
354.         if (!horizontalTemplate || _project.VideoResolution.Width <= 0 || _project.VideoResolution.Height <= 0)
355.             return (clickX, clickY, null);
356. 
357.         bool fromLeft = clickX < 0.5;
358.         double halfWNorm = Math.Min(0.5, (template.Width / 2.0) / _project.VideoResolution.Width);
359.         double halfHNorm = Math.Min(0.5, (template.Height / 2.0) / _project.VideoResolution.Height);
360.         double centerX = fromLeft ? halfWNorm : 1.0 - halfWNorm;
361.         double centerY = Math.Clamp(clickY, halfHNorm, 1.0 - halfHNorm);
362.         var sideStyle = fromLeft ? AnimationStyle.SlideLeft : AnimationStyle.SlideRight;
363. 
364.         var overrideAnim = CloneAnimation(anim);
365.         if (IsHorizontalSlide(overrideAnim.Enter)) overrideAnim.Enter = sideStyle;
366.         if (IsHorizontalSlide(overrideAnim.Exit)) overrideAnim.Exit = sideStyle;
367.         return (centerX, centerY, overrideAnim);
368.     }
369. 
370.     private async Task CheckDependenciesAsync(bool promptIfMissing)
371.     {
372.         try
373.         {
374.             var statuses = await _api.Dependencies.CheckAsync();
375.             var missing = statuses.Where(s => s.State != DependencyState.Installed).Select(s => s.Name).ToList();
376.             InstallDepsButton.IsVisible = missing.Count > 0;
377.             if (missing.Count == 0) return;
378.             if (!promptIfMissing) return;
379.             var confirm = await ConfirmDialog.ShowAsync(this,
380.                 "FFmpeg required",
381.                 $"VideoEmpty needs FFmpeg. Missing: {string.Join(", ", missing)}.\nInstall now?");
382.             if (confirm) await InstallDepsAsync();
383.         }
384.         catch (Exception ex)
385.         {
386.             Log.Error("UI", "Dependency check failed", ex);
387.             VideoInfoLabel.Text = $"Dependency check failed: {ex.Message}";
388.         }
389.     }
390. 
391.     private async void OnInstallDeps(object? sender, RoutedEventArgs e) => await InstallDepsAsync();
392. 
393.     private async Task InstallDepsAsync()
394.     {
395.         InstallDepsButton.IsEnabled = false;
396.         var progress = new Progress<DependencyInstallProgress>(p => ExportStatus.Text = $"Install {p.Name}: {p.Stage} {p.Detail}".Trim());
397.         try
398.         {
399.             await _api.Dependencies.InstallMissingAsync(progress);
400.             await CheckDependenciesAsync(promptIfMissing: false);
401.             ExportStatus.Text = "Install complete.";
402.         }
403.         catch (Exception ex)
404.         {
405.             Log.Error("UI", "Install failed", ex);
406.             ExportStatus.Text = $"Install failed: {ex.Message}";
407.         }
408.         finally
409.         {
410.             InstallDepsButton.IsEnabled = true;
411.         }
412.     }

555.     private void UpdateInstanceEditor()
556.     {

559.             InstanceEditor.IsVisible = false;
560.             _instanceEditorInstanceId = null;

562.             return;

567.         _isApplyingInstanceEditor = true;

569.         {

571.             InstanceDurationBox.Text = i.DurationMs.ToString();

574.             var template = _project.Templates.FirstOrDefault(t => t.Id == i.TemplateId);
575.             InstanceTemplateNameLabel.Text = template is not null ? $"Template: {template.Name}" : $"Template ID: {i.TemplateId}";
576.

578.             if (template is not null)
579.             {
580.                 int row = 1;
581.                 foreach (var te in template.Elements.OfType<TextElement>())
582.                 {
583.                     InstanceTextFields.Add(new InstanceTextFieldItem
584.                     {
585.                         ElementId = te.Id,
586.                         Label = $"Row {row++}",
587.                         Value = i.TextValues.TryGetValue(te.Id, out var v) ? v : te.DefaultText
588.                     });
589.                 }
590.             }
591.             else
592.             {
593.                 foreach (var kv in i.TextValues)
594.                 {
595.                     InstanceTextFields.Add(new InstanceTextFieldItem
596.                     {
597.                         ElementId = kv.Key,
598.                         Label = kv.Key,
599.                         Value = kv.Value
600.                     });
601.                 }
602.             }
603.         }
604.         finally
605.         {
606.             _isApplyingInstanceEditor = false;
607.         }
608.     }

1557.         ExpandRightEdgeButton.IsVisible = inEditor && !_showRightPanel;

435.     private void RefreshInstances(string? preserveSelectedId = null)
436.     {
437.         preserveSelectedId ??= (InstancesList.SelectedItem as InstanceListItem)?.Instance.Id;
438.         Instances.Clear();
439.         foreach (var i in _api.ListInstances(_project).OrderBy(i => i.StartMs))
440.         {
441.             var templateName = _project.Templates.FirstOrDefault(t => t.Id == i.TemplateId)?.Name ?? i.TemplateId;
442.             var startTs = TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(i.StartMs);
443.             Instances.Add(new InstanceListItem
444.             {
445.                 Instance = i,
446.                 TemplateName = templateName,
447.                 TimeLabel = $"{(int)startTs.TotalMinutes}:{startTs.Seconds:00}.{startTs.Milliseconds:000}",
448.                 TextPreview = BuildTextPreview(i)
449.             });
450.         }
451. 
452.         if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(preserveSelectedId))
453.             InstancesList.SelectedItem = Instances.FirstOrDefault(x => x.Instance.Id == preserveSelectedId);
454.     }

456.     private static string BuildTextPreview(TemplateInstance i)
457.     {
458.         if (i.TextValues is null || i.TextValues.Count == 0) return string.Empty;
459.         var joined = string.Join(" / ",
460.             i.TextValues.Values
461.                 .Where(v => !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(v))
462.                 .Select(v => v.Replace('\r', ' ').Replace('\n', ' ').Trim()));
463.         if (joined.Length > 80) joined = joined.Substring(0, 79) + "…";
464.         return joined;
465.     }

471.     {

473.         {
474.             Title = "Open video",
475.             AllowMultiple = false,
476.             FileTypeFilter = new[] { new FilePickerFileType("Video") { Patterns = new[] { "*.mp4", "*.mov", "*.mkv", "*.avi", "*.webm" } } }

478.         var path = files.FirstOrDefault()?.TryGetLocalPath();
479.         if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(path)) return;
480. 
481.         try
482.         {
483.             _project = await _api.SetVideoAsync(_project, path);

485.             TimeSlider.Value = 0;
486.             VideoInfoLabel.Text = $"{Path.GetFileName(path)} • {_project.VideoResolution.Width}x{_project.VideoResolution.Height} @ {_project.VideoFps:0.##} fps • {_project.VideoDurationMs / 1000.0:0.0}s";
487.             await RefreshPreviewAsync();
488.             await AutoSaveAsync("set-video");

490.         catch (Exception ex)

1. using System;
2. using System.Collections.Generic;
3. using System.IO;
4. using System.Linq;
5. using System.Security.Cryptography;
6. using System.Text;
7. using System.Text.Json;
8. 
9. namespace VideoEmpty.UI;
10. 
11. public sealed class UiSettings
12. {
13.     public List<string> RecentProjects { get; set; } = new();
14.     public bool AutoDeleteBackupsEnabled { get; set; }
15.     public int AutoDeleteBackupsDays { get; set; } = 90;
16.     public bool SnapToGridEnabled { get; set; }
17.     public int SnapGridDivisions { get; set; } = 10;
18. }
19. 
20. public static class UiSettingsStore
21. {
22.     private static readonly JsonSerializerOptions JsonOptions = new() { WriteIndented = true };
23. 
24.     public static string AppDataDir
25.     {
26.         get
27.         {
28.             var baseDir = Environment.GetFolderPath(Environment.SpecialFolder.LocalApplicationData);
29.             var dir = Path.Combine(baseDir, "VideoEmpty");
30.             Directory.CreateDirectory(dir);
31.             return dir;
32.         }
33.     }
34. 
35.     public static string SettingsPath => Path.Combine(AppDataDir, "ui-settings.json");
36. 
37.     public static UiSettings Load()
38.     {
39.         try
40.         {
41.             if (!File.Exists(SettingsPath)) return new UiSettings();
42.             return JsonSerializer.Deserialize<UiSettings>(File.ReadAllText(SettingsPath), JsonOptions) ?? new UiSettings();
43.         }
44.         catch
45.         {
46.             return new UiSettings();
47.         }
48.     }
49. 
50.     public static void Save(UiSettings settings)
51.     {
52.         File.WriteAllText(SettingsPath, JsonSerializer.Serialize(settings, JsonOptions));
53.     }
54. 
55.     public static string EnsureProjectRoot()
56.     {
57.         var dir = Path.Combine(AppDataDir, "projects");
58.         Directory.CreateDirectory(dir);
59.         return dir;
60.     }
61. 
62.     public static string GetProjectPath(string projectName)
63.     {
64.         var safe = string.Join("_", projectName.Split(Path.GetInvalidFileNameChars(), StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries)).Trim();
65.         if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(safe)) safe = "project";
66.         return Path.Combine(EnsureProjectRoot(), $"{safe}.veproj");
67.     }
68. 
69.     public static string GetBackupDir(string projectPath)
70.     {
71.         var hash = Convert.ToHexString(SHA256.HashData(Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(projectPath))).Substring(0, 10);
72.         var safe = Path.GetFileNameWithoutExtension(projectPath);
73.         var dir = Path.Combine(AppDataDir, "backups", $"{safe}_{hash}");
74.         Directory.CreateDirectory(dir);
75.         return dir;
76.     }
77. }
78.
```

</details>

1. using System;
2. using Avalonia.Controls;
3. using Avalonia.Layout;
4. 
5. namespace VideoEmpty.UI;
6. 
7. public sealed class SettingsDialog : Window
8. {
9.     private readonly CheckBox _enableDelete;
10.     private readonly TextBox _daysBox;
11.     private readonly CheckBox _snapToGrid;
12.     private readonly TextBox _snapDivisionsBox;
13. 
14.     public bool? EnableAutoDelete { get; private set; }
15.     public int? AutoDeleteDays { get; private set; }
16.     public bool? SnapToGridEnabled { get; private set; }
17.     public int? SnapGridDivisions { get; private set; }
18. 
19.     public SettingsDialog(UiSettings settings)
20.     {
21.         Title = "Settings";
22.         Width = 420;
23.         Height = 300;
24. 
25.         _enableDelete = new CheckBox
26.         {
27.             Content = "Auto-delete backup files after N days",
28.             IsChecked = settings.AutoDeleteBackupsEnabled
29.         };
30.         _daysBox = new TextBox { Text = settings.AutoDeleteBackupsDays.ToString(), Width = 80 };
31.         _snapToGrid = new CheckBox
32.         {
33.             Content = "Enable snap to grid for template placement",
34.             IsChecked = settings.SnapToGridEnabled
35.         };
36.         _snapDivisionsBox = new TextBox { Text = Math.Max(2, settings.SnapGridDivisions).ToString(), Width = 80 };
37. 
38.         var ok = new Button { Content = "Save", IsDefault = true };
39.         var cancel = new Button { Content = "Cancel", IsCancel = true };
40. 
41.         ok.Click += (_, _) =>
42.         {
43.             EnableAutoDelete = _enableDelete.IsChecked == true;
44.             AutoDeleteDays = int.TryParse(_daysBox.Text, out var d) ? Math.Max(1, d) : 90;
45.             SnapToGridEnabled = _snapToGrid.IsChecked == true;
46.             SnapGridDivisions = int.TryParse(_snapDivisionsBox.Text, out var snapDivisions)
47.                 ? Math.Max(2, snapDivisions)
48.                 : 10;
49.             Close(true);
50.         };
51.         cancel.Click += (_, _) => Close(false);
52. 
53.         Content = new StackPanel
54.         {
55.             Margin = new Avalonia.Thickness(12),
56.             Spacing = 10,
57.             Children =
58.             {
59.                 _enableDelete,
60.                 new StackPanel
61.                 {
62.                     Orientation = Orientation.Horizontal,
63.                     Spacing = 8,
64.                     Children = { new TextBlock { Text = "Days:" }, _daysBox }
65.                 },
66.                 _snapToGrid,
67.                 new StackPanel
68.                 {
69.                     Orientation = Orientation.Horizontal,
70.                     Spacing = 8,
71.                     Children =
72.                     {
73.                         new TextBlock { Text = "Snap points per axis:" },
74.                         _snapDivisionsBox,
75.                         new TextBlock { Text = "(10 = width/height split into 10 steps)" }
76.                     }
77.                 },
78.                 new StackPanel
79.                 {
80.                     Orientation = Orientation.Horizontal,
81.                     HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Right,
82.                     Spacing = 8,
83.                     Children = { ok, cancel }
84.                 }
85.             }
86.         };
87.     }
88. }
89.

348.     private static bool IsHorizontalSlide(AnimationStyle style) =>
349.         style is AnimationStyle.SlideLeft or AnimationStyle.SlideRight;
350. 
351.     private static Animation CloneAnimation(Animation source) => new()
352.     {
353.         Enter = source.Enter,
354.         Exit = source.Exit,
355.         EnterMs = source.EnterMs,
356.         ExitMs = source.ExitMs
357.     };
358. 
359.     private (double centerX, double centerY, Animation? animationOverride) ResolveClickPlacement(
360.         Template template, double clickX, double clickY)
361.     {
362.         (clickX, clickY) = ApplySnapToGrid(clickX, clickY);
363.         var anim = template.Animation;
364.         bool horizontalTemplate = IsHorizontalSlide(anim.Enter) || IsHorizontalSlide(anim.Exit);
365.         if (!horizontalTemplate || _project.VideoResolution.Width <= 0 || _project.VideoResolution.Height <= 0)
366.             return (clickX, clickY, null);
367. 
368.         bool fromLeft = clickX < 0.5;
369.         double halfWNorm = Math.Min(0.5, (template.Width / 2.0) / _project.VideoResolution.Width);
370.         double halfHNorm = Math.Min(0.5, (template.Height / 2.0) / _project.VideoResolution.Height);
371.         double centerX = fromLeft ? halfWNorm : 1.0 - halfWNorm;
372.         double centerY = Math.Clamp(clickY, halfHNorm, 1.0 - halfHNorm);
373.         var sideStyle = fromLeft ? AnimationStyle.SlideLeft : AnimationStyle.SlideRight;
374. 
375.         var overrideAnim = CloneAnimation(anim);
376.         if (IsHorizontalSlide(overrideAnim.Enter)) overrideAnim.Enter = sideStyle;
377.         if (IsHorizontalSlide(overrideAnim.Exit)) overrideAnim.Exit = sideStyle;
378.         return (centerX, centerY, overrideAnim);
379.     }

532.     private (double x, double y) GetNormalizedPreviewPoint(Avalonia.Point pos)
533.     {
534.         double width = Math.Max(1, PreviewImage.Bounds.Width);
535.         double height = Math.Max(1, PreviewImage.Bounds.Height);
536.         double cx = Math.Clamp(pos.X / width, 0, 1);
537.         double cy = Math.Clamp(pos.Y / height, 0, 1);
538.         return (cx, cy);
539.     }
540. 
541.     private (double x, double y) ApplySnapToGrid(double x, double y)
542.     {
543.         if (!_settings.SnapToGridEnabled) return (x, y);
544.         int divisions = Math.Max(2, _settings.SnapGridDivisions);
545.         double step = 1.0 / divisions;
546.         double snappedX = Math.Clamp(Math.Round(x / step) * step, 0, 1);
547.         double snappedY = Math.Clamp(Math.Round(y / step) * step, 0, 1);
548.         return (snappedX, snappedY);
549.     }

570.     private void RenderPlacementOverlay()
571.     {
572.         PlacementOverlay.Children.Clear();
573. 
574.         if (PreviewImage.Source is null) return;
575.         double width = PreviewImage.Bounds.Width;
576.         double height = PreviewImage.Bounds.Height;
577.         if (width <= 1 || height <= 1) return;
578. 
579.         PlacementOverlay.Width = width;
580.         PlacementOverlay.Height = height;
581. 
582.         if (_settings.SnapToGridEnabled)
583.         {
584.             int divisions = Math.Max(2, _settings.SnapGridDivisions);
585.             var gridBrush = new AvaloniaMedia.SolidColorBrush(AvaloniaMedia.Color.FromArgb(70, 255, 255, 255));
586.             for (int i = 1; i < divisions; i++)
587.             {
588.                 double x = i * width / divisions;
589.                 double y = i * height / divisions;
590.                 PlacementOverlay.Children.Add(new Line
591.                 {
592.                     StartPoint = new Avalonia.Point(x, 0),
593.                     EndPoint = new Avalonia.Point(x, height),
594.                     Stroke = gridBrush,
595.                     StrokeThickness = 1
596.                 });
597.                 PlacementOverlay.Children.Add(new Line
598.                 {
599.                     StartPoint = new Avalonia.Point(0, y),
600.                     EndPoint = new Avalonia.Point(width, y),
601.                     Stroke = gridBrush,
602.                     StrokeThickness = 1
603.                 });
604.             }
605.         }
606. 
607.         if (_armedTemplateId is null || _previewHoverNormalized is not { } hover) return;
608.         var template = _project.Templates.FirstOrDefault(t => t.Id == _armedTemplateId);
609.         if (template is null) return;
610. 
611.         var placement = ResolveClickPlacement(template, hover.x, hover.y);
612.         double centerX = placement.centerX * width;
613.         double centerY = placement.centerY * height;
614. 
615.         double previewWidth = _project.VideoResolution.Width > 0
616.             ? (template.Width / (double)_project.VideoResolution.Width) * width
617.             : 0;
618.         double previewHeight = _project.VideoResolution.Height > 0
619.             ? (template.Height / (double)_project.VideoResolution.Height) * height
620.             : 0;
621. 
622.         previewWidth = Math.Clamp(previewWidth, 4, width);
623.         previewHeight = Math.Clamp(previewHeight, 4, height);
624. 
625.         var rect = new Rectangle
626.         {
627.             Width = previewWidth,
628.             Height = previewHeight,
629.             StrokeThickness = 2,
630.             Stroke = new AvaloniaMedia.SolidColorBrush(AvaloniaMedia.Color.FromArgb(220, 72, 197, 255)),
631.             Fill = new AvaloniaMedia.SolidColorBrush(AvaloniaMedia.Color.FromArgb(40, 72, 197, 255)),
632.             RadiusX = 4,
633.             RadiusY = 4
634.         };
635.         Canvas.SetLeft(rect, centerX - previewWidth / 2);
636.         Canvas.SetTop(rect, centerY - previewHeight / 2);
637.         PlacementOverlay.Children.Add(rect);
638. 
639.         var centerDot = new Ellipse
640.         {
641.             Width = 8,
642.             Height = 8,
643.             Fill = new AvaloniaMedia.SolidColorBrush(AvaloniaMedia.Color.FromArgb(255, 72, 197, 255))
644.         };
645.         Canvas.SetLeft(centerDot, centerX - 4);
646.         Canvas.SetTop(centerDot, centerY - 4);
647.         PlacementOverlay.Children.Add(centerDot);
648.     }
649. 
650.     private async void OnPreviewClicked(object? sender, PointerPressedEventArgs e)
651.     {
652.         if (_armedTemplateId is null || PreviewImage.Source is null) return;
653.         var (cx, cy) = GetNormalizedPreviewPoint(e.GetPosition(PreviewImage));
654. 
655.         if (_playTimer is { IsEnabled: true }) TogglePlay();
656. 
657.         var template = _api.GetTemplate(_project, _armedTemplateId);
658.         var placement = ResolveClickPlacement(template, cx, cy);
659.         var values = template.Elements.OfType<TextElement>().ToDictionary(t => t.Id, t => t.DefaultText ?? "");
660.         var inst = _api.AddInstance(_project, new AddInstanceRequest(template.Id, placement.centerX, placement.centerY, _currentTimeMs, null, values, placement.animationOverride));
661.         RefreshInstances(inst.Id);
662.         InstancesList.SelectedItem = Instances.FirstOrDefault(item => item.Instance.Id == inst.Id);
663.         if (_compactMode)
664.         {
665.             ShowCompactOverlayForCurrentInstance(template);
666.         }
667.         else
668.         {
669.             FocusFirstInstanceTextFieldForReplace();
670.         }
671.         await RefreshPreviewAsync();
672.         await AutoSaveAsync("add-instance");
673.     }

60. ## Project file format
61. 
62. `.veproj` — pretty-printed JSON; round-trips through `ProjectJson`. Element types use a `kind` discriminator (`shape` / `text`).
63. 
64. ## How placement works
65. 
66. 1. Pause / scrub to the desired time.
67. 2. Click a template in the left palette (it becomes "armed").
68. 3. Click on the video preview → text dialog appears (multi-line; lines map to text elements top-to-bottom).
69. 4. The instance is added with its **center** at the click point (or nearest snap point when snap-to-grid is enabled) and the template's default duration.
70. 5. Edit start/duration/position/text in the right panel; export to MP4 when done.
71. 
72. The **Settings** dialog includes placement options for **Snap to grid** and **Snap points per axis** (e.g. `10` splits width/height into 10 steps). When a template is armed, the preview shows a live placement box on the video frame before you click.
73.
```

</details>

1781.     private async Task PollJob(string jobId)
1782.     {
1783.         while (true)
1784.         {
1785.             await Task.Delay(500);
1786.             var s = _api.GetJobStatus(jobId);
1787.             ExportStatus.Text = $"Job {jobId[..8]}: {s.State} ({s.Progress * 100:0}%) {s.Message}";
1788.             if (s.State is JobState.Completed or JobState.Failed or JobState.Cancelled)
1789.             {
1790.                 if (s.State == JobState.Failed) ExportStatus.Text += " — " + s.Error;
1791.                 else if (s.State == JobState.Completed) ExportStatus.Text = $"Done → {s.OutputPath}";
1792.                 break;
1793.             }
1794.         }
1795.     }
```

</details>

5. Updated README placement section to document snap behavior and live placement preview.

Implemented the placement UX update in the UI.

**What changed**
1. Added new settings persisted in `UiSettings`: `SnapToGridEnabled` and `SnapGridDivisions` (default `10`).
2. Extended `SettingsDialog` with:
   - **Enable snap to grid** toggle

You should **install .NET SDK 8.0.319** and keep the project pinned as-is.

The repo is explicitly pinned to `8.0.319` in `global.json`, and every project targets `net8.0`. Changing the SDK version in-project is a migration decision; this error is just an environment mismatch.

Use one of these on Windows:

```powershell
winget install Microsoft.DotNet.SDK.8 --version 8.0.319
```

or download installer directly from Microsoft’s .NET 8 SDK page, then verify:

```powershell
dotnet --list-sdks
```

You should see `8.0.319` installed, after which `dotnet build`/`dotnet test` will resolve correctly under the current `global.json`.

I installed **.NET SDK 8.0.319** (the version pinned in `global.json`), so the NETSDK1141 error is resolved without changing project SDK policy.

I also fixed a compile issue introduced during the snap-to-grid preview work (`Path` ambiguity from Avalonia shape imports) by aliasing the shape types (`Line`, `Rectangle`, `Ellipse`) in `MainWindow.axaml.cs`.

`dotnet build` now succeeds and `dotnet test` passes for all test projects.

### ✓ Task Complete

Completed end-to-end.

I installed **.NET SDK 8.0.319** (the version pinned in `global.json`), so the NETSDK1141 error is resolved without changing project SDK policy.

I also fixed a compile issue introduced during the snap-to-grid preview work (`Path` ambiguity from Avalonia shape imports) by aliasing the shape types (`Line`, `Rectangle`, `Ellipse`) in `MainWindow.axaml.cs`.

`dotnet build` now succeeds and `dotnet test` passes for all test projects.

350.     private static bool IsHorizontalSlide(AnimationStyle style) =>
351.         style is AnimationStyle.SlideLeft or AnimationStyle.SlideRight;
352. 
353.     private static Animation CloneAnimation(Animation source) => new()
354.     {
355.         Enter = source.Enter,
356.         Exit = source.Exit,
357.         EnterMs = source.EnterMs,
358.         ExitMs = source.ExitMs
359.     };

361.     private (double centerX, double centerY, Animation? animationOverride) ResolveClickPlacement(
362.         Template template, double clickX, double clickY)
363.     {
364.         (clickX, clickY) = ApplySnapToGrid(clickX, clickY);
365.         var anim = template.Animation;
366.         bool horizontalTemplate = IsHorizontalSlide(anim.Enter) || IsHorizontalSlide(anim.Exit);
367.         if (!horizontalTemplate || _project.VideoResolution.Width <= 0 || _project.VideoResolution.Height <= 0)
368.             return (clickX, clickY, null);
369. 
370.         bool fromLeft = clickX < 0.5;
371.         double halfWNorm = Math.Min(0.5, (template.Width / 2.0) / _project.VideoResolution.Width);
372.         double halfHNorm = Math.Min(0.5, (template.Height / 2.0) / _project.VideoResolution.Height);
373.         double centerX = fromLeft ? halfWNorm : 1.0 - halfWNorm;
374.         double centerY = Math.Clamp(clickY, halfHNorm, 1.0 - halfHNorm);
375.         var sideStyle = fromLeft ? AnimationStyle.SlideLeft : AnimationStyle.SlideRight;
376. 
377.         var overrideAnim = CloneAnimation(anim);
378.         if (IsHorizontalSlide(overrideAnim.Enter)) overrideAnim.Enter = sideStyle;
379.         if (IsHorizontalSlide(overrideAnim.Exit)) overrideAnim.Exit = sideStyle;
380.         return (centerX, centerY, overrideAnim);
381.     }
382. 
383.     private async Task CheckDependenciesAsync(bool promptIfMissing)
384.     {
385.         try
386.         {
387.             var statuses = await _api.Dependencies.CheckAsync();
388.             var missing = statuses.Where(s => s.State != DependencyState.Installed).Select(s => s.Name).ToList();
389.             InstallDepsButton.IsVisible = missing.Count > 0;
390.             if (missing.Count == 0) return;
391.             if (!promptIfMissing) return;
392.             var confirm = await ConfirmDialog.ShowAsync(this,
393.                 "FFmpeg required",
394.                 $"VideoEmpty needs FFmpeg. Missing: {string.Join(", ", missing)}.\nInstall now?");
395.             if (confirm) await InstallDepsAsync();
396.         }
397.         catch (Exception ex)
398.         {
399.             Log.Error("UI", "Dependency check failed", ex);
400.             VideoInfoLabel.Text = $"Dependency check failed: {ex.Message}";
401.         }
402.     }
403. 
404.     private async void OnInstallDeps(object? sender, RoutedEventArgs e) => await InstallDepsAsync();
405. 
406.     private async Task InstallDepsAsync()
407.     {
408.         InstallDepsButton.IsEnabled = false;
409.         var progress = new Progress<DependencyInstallProgress>(p => ExportStatus.Text = $"Install {p.Name}: {p.Stage} {p.Detail}".Trim());
410.         try
411.         {
412.             await _api.Dependencies.InstallMissingAsync(progress);
413.             await CheckDependenciesAsync(promptIfMissing: false);
414.             ExportStatus.Text = "Install complete.";
415.         }
416.         catch (Exception ex)
417.         {
418.             Log.Error("UI", "Install failed", ex);
419.             ExportStatus.Text = $"Install failed: {ex.Message}";
420.         }
421.         finally
422.         {
423.             InstallDepsButton.IsEnabled = true;
424.         }
425.     }

427.     private static void OpenInShell(string path)
428.     {
429.         try
430.         {
431.             var dir = Path.GetDirectoryName(path) ?? path;
432.             if (OperatingSystem.IsWindows())
433.                 Process.Start(new ProcessStartInfo("explorer.exe", $"\"{dir}\"") { UseShellExecute = true });
434.             else if (OperatingSystem.IsMacOS())
435.                 Process.Start("open", dir);
436.             else
437.                 Process.Start("xdg-open", dir);
438.         }
439.         catch (Exception ex) { Log.Error("UI", "Open folder failed", ex); }
440.     }

192.     private async void OnNewProject(object? sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
193.     {
194.         var defaultName = $"{DateTime.Today:yyyy-MM-dd}-Project";
195.         var dlg = new TextEntryDialog("Project name", "New Project", defaultName);
196.         var input = await dlg.ShowDialog<string?>(this);
197.         if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(input)) return;
198. 
199.         var name = input.Trim();
200.         var p = _api.CreateProject(name);
201.         var path = UiSettingsStore.GetProjectPath(name);
202.         ApplyProject(p, path);
203.         RememberRecentProject(path);
204.         await AutoSaveAsync("new-project");

23. /// Windows -> winget (Gyan.FFmpeg), macOS -> Homebrew (brew install ffmpeg).

26. {

31.     bool HasMissing { get; }

36.     /// manager itself is missing.

38.     Task InstallMissingAsync(IProgress<DependencyInstallProgress>? progress = null, CancellationToken ct = default);
39. }

```
Implemented the dashboard-first dependency UX.

**What changed**
1. Replaced the startup dependency confirmation dialog with an in-window dashboard prompt.
2. Added a dashboard dependency panel (`DashboardDependencyPanel`) with:
   - missing-library message
   - install button (`DashboardInstallDepsButton`)
   - install status text
   - install progress bar shown during installation
3. Wired dashboard install flow to the existing installer so progress updates are shown live in the dashboard.
4. When dependencies are missing, dashboard actions are disabled (`New Project`, `Open Project`, `Open Log`, and recent projects list interaction) until installation is complete.
5. Dependency state now updates dashboard controls and prompt visibility directly from `CheckDependenciesAsync`/`InstallDepsAsync`.

Files updated:
- `src\VideoEmpty.UI\MainWindow.axaml`
- `src\VideoEmpty.UI\MainWindow.axaml.cs`
```

</details>

<sub>⏱️ 17m 4s</sub>

### ✓ Task Complete

Implemented the dashboard-first dependency UX.

**What changed**
1. Replaced the startup dependency confirmation dialog with an in-window dashboard prompt.
2. Added a dashboard dependency panel (`DashboardDependencyPanel`) with:
   - missing-library message
   - install button (`DashboardInstallDepsButton`)
   - install status text
   - install progress bar shown during installation
3. Wired dashboard install flow to the existing installer so progress updates are shown live in the dashboard.
4. When dependencies are missing, dashboard actions are disabled (`New Project`, `Open Project`, `Open Log`, and recent projects list interaction) until installation is complete.
5. Dependency state now updates dashboard controls and prompt visibility directly from `CheckDependenciesAsync`/`InstallDepsAsync`.

Files updated:
- `src\VideoEmpty.UI\MainWindow.axaml`
- `src\VideoEmpty.UI\MainWindow.axaml.cs`

<sub>⏱️ 23m 32s</sub>

### ✅ `view`

**view the file at D:\Source\Repos\VideoEmpty\src\VideoEmpty.Rendering\FFmpeg\FFmpegDependencyManager.cs.**

D:\Source\Repos\VideoEmpty\src\VideoEmpty.Rendering\FFmpeg\FFmpegDependencyManager.cs

```
1. using System.Diagnostics;
2. using VideoEmpty.Core.Api;
3. using VideoEmpty.Core.Diagnostics;
4. 
5. namespace VideoEmpty.Rendering.FFmpeg;
6. 
7. /// <summary>
8. /// Detects ffmpeg/ffprobe and installs them via the platform package manager
9. /// (winget on Windows, Homebrew on macOS). Linux is supported as best-effort
10. /// (apt-get on Debian/Ubuntu, dnf on Fedora) but otherwise reports an actionable error.
11. /// </summary>
12. public sealed class FFmpegDependencyManager : IDependencyManager
13. {
14.     private readonly Func<FFmpegBinaries> _rediscover;
15.     private FFmpegBinaries _bin;
16. 
17.     public FFmpegDependencyManager(Func<FFmpegBinaries>? rediscover = null)
18.     {
19.         _rediscover = rediscover ?? FFmpegBinaries.Discover;
20.         _bin = _rediscover();
21.     }
22. 
23.     /// <summary>Currently-known binary locations (refreshed on Check/Install).</summary>
24.     public FFmpegBinaries Binaries => _bin;
25. 
26.     public bool HasMissing => !_bin.FFmpegFound || !_bin.FFprobeFound;
27. 
28.     public async Task<IReadOnlyList<DependencyStatus>> CheckAsync(CancellationToken ct = default)
29.     {
30.         _bin = _rediscover();
31.         var list = new List<DependencyStatus>();
32.         list.Add(await BuildStatusAsync("ffmpeg",  _bin.FFmpegPath,  _bin.FFmpegFound,  ct));
33.         list.Add(await BuildStatusAsync("ffprobe", _bin.FFprobePath, _bin.FFprobeFound, ct));
34.         return list;
35.     }
36. 
37.     private static async Task<DependencyStatus> BuildStatusAsync(string name, string path, bool found, CancellationToken ct)
38.     {
39.         var s = new DependencyStatus { Name = name, Path = path, State = found ? DependencyState.Installed : DependencyState.Missing };
40.         if (found)
41.         {
42.             try
43.             {
44.                 var (stdout, _, code) = await ProcessHelper.RunAsync(path, "-version", ct);
45.                 if (code == 0)
46.                 {
47.                     var firstLine = stdout.Split('\n').FirstOrDefault()?.Trim();
48.                     s.Version = firstLine;
49.                 }
50.             }
51.             catch (Exception ex)
52.             {
53.                 s.Message = ex.Message;
54.             }
55.         }
56.         else
57.         {
58.             s.Message = $"'{name}' not found on PATH.";
59.         }
60.         return s;
61.     }
62. 
63.     public async Task InstallMissingAsync(IProgress<DependencyInstallProgress>? progress = null, CancellationToken ct = default)
64.     {
65.         await CheckAsync(ct);
66.         if (!HasMissing) return;
67. 
68.         if (OperatingSystem.IsWindows())
69.             await InstallWindowsAsync(progress, ct);
70.         else if (OperatingSystem.IsMacOS())
71.             await InstallMacAsync(progress, ct);
72.         else if (OperatingSystem.IsLinux())
73.             await InstallLinuxAsync(progress, ct);
74.         else
75.             throw new PlatformNotSupportedException("Automatic install is not supported on this OS.");
76. 
77.         // Re-discover after install so subsequent calls see the new binaries.
78.         _bin = _rediscover();
79.         if (HasMissing)
80.             throw new InvalidOperationException(
81.                 "FFmpeg installation completed but the binaries were still not detected. " +
82.                 "You may need to restart the app so a new PATH is picked up.");
83.     }
84. 
85.     // -------------------- Windows (winget) --------------------
86.     private async Task InstallWindowsAsync(IProgress<DependencyInstallProgress>? progress, CancellationToken ct)
87.     {
88.         var winget = LocateExecutable("winget");
89.         if (winget is null)
90.             throw new InvalidOperationException(
91.                 "winget (Windows Package Manager) was not found. Install 'App Installer' from the Microsoft Store " +
92.                 "(https://aka.ms/getwinget), then retry. As an alternative, install FFmpeg manually from https://www.gyan.dev/ffmpeg/builds/ " +
93.                 "and add the bin folder to PATH, or set VIDEOEMPTY_FFMPEG / VIDEOEMPTY_FFPROBE.");
94. 
95.         progress?.Report(new DependencyInstallProgress { Name = "ffmpeg", Stage = "winget install Gyan.FFmpeg" });
96.         Log.Info("DepInstall", "Running winget install Gyan.FFmpeg");
97.         var args = "install --id=Gyan.FFmpeg -e --silent --accept-source-agreements --accept-package-agreements";
98.         var (stdout, stderr, code) = await ProcessHelper.RunAsync(winget, args, ct,
99.             line => progress?.Report(new DependencyInstallProgress { Name = "ffmpeg", Stage = "installing", Detail = line }));
100.         Log.Info("winget", $"exit={code}\nstdout:\n{stdout}\nstderr:\n{stderr}");
101.         if (code != 0)
102.             throw new InvalidOperationException($"winget failed (exit {code}): {(string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(stderr) ? stdout : stderr)}");
103. 
104.         // winget alters PATH for new processes, but the current process won't see it.
105.         // Try to locate the freshly installed binaries and update PATH for this process.
106.         TryRefreshProcessPathFromRegistry();
107.         var refreshed = FFmpegBinaries.Discover();
108.         if (!refreshed.FFmpegFound || !refreshed.FFprobeFound)
109.         {
110.             // Search common winget link locations.
111.             var hints = new[]
112.             {
113.                 Path.Combine(Environment.GetFolderPath(Environment.SpecialFolder.LocalApplicationData), "Microsoft", "WinGet", "Links"),
114.                 Path.Combine(Environment.GetFolderPath(Environment.SpecialFolder.LocalApplicationData), "Microsoft", "WindowsApps")
115.             };
116.             AddPaths(hints);
117.         }
118.     }
119. 
120.     private static void TryRefreshProcessPathFromRegistry()
121.     {
122.         if (!OperatingSystem.IsWindows()) return;
123.         try
124.         {
125.             var machine = Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("PATH", EnvironmentVariableTarget.Machine) ?? "";
126.             var user    = Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("PATH", EnvironmentVariableTarget.User) ?? "";
127.             var combined = string.Join(Path.PathSeparator, new[] { user, machine, Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("PATH") ?? "" }
128.                 .Where(s => !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(s)));
129.             Environment.SetEnvironmentVariable("PATH", combined);
130.             Log.Info("DepInstall", "Refreshed process PATH from registry.");
131.         }
132.         catch (Exception ex) { Log.Warn("DepInstall", "Refresh PATH failed: " + ex.Message); }
133.     }
134. 
135.     private static void AddPaths(IEnumerable<string> dirs)
136.     {
137.         var current = Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("PATH") ?? "";
138.         var parts = current.Split(Path.PathSeparator, StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries).ToList();
139.         foreach (var d in dirs)
140.             if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(d) && !parts.Contains(d, StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
141.                 parts.Add(d);
142.         Environment.SetEnvironmentVariable("PATH", string.Join(Path.PathSeparator, parts));
143.     }
144. 
145.     // -------------------- macOS (Homebrew) --------------------
146.     private async Task InstallMacAsync(IProgress<DependencyInstallProgress>? progress, CancellationToken ct)
147.     {
148.         var brew = LocateExecutable("brew") ?? "/opt/homebrew/bin/brew";
149.         if (!File.Exists(brew))
150.             brew = "/usr/local/bin/brew";
151.         if (!File.Exists(brew))
152.             throw new InvalidOperationException(
153.                 "Homebrew was not found. Install it from https://brew.sh (one line in Terminal), then retry. " +
154.                 "Alternatively, install FFmpeg manually and ensure 'ffmpeg' / 'ffprobe' are on PATH, or set " +
155.                 "VIDEOEMPTY_FFMPEG / VIDEOEMPTY_FFPROBE.");
156. 
157.         progress?.Report(new DependencyInstallProgress { Name = "ffmpeg", Stage = "brew install ffmpeg" });
158.         Log.Info("DepInstall", $"Running {brew} install ffmpeg");
159.         var (stdout, stderr, code) = await ProcessHelper.RunAsync(brew, "install ffmpeg", ct,
160.             line => progress?.Report(new DependencyInstallProgress { Name = "ffmpeg", Stage = "installing", Detail = line }));
161.         Log.Info("brew", $"exit={code}\nstdout:\n{stdout}\nstderr:\n{stderr}");
162.         if (code != 0)
163.             throw new InvalidOperationException($"brew failed (exit {code}): {(string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(stderr) ? stdout : stderr)}");
164. 
165.         // Add Homebrew bin dirs to process PATH so this run can locate ffmpeg.
166.         AddPaths(new[] { "/opt/homebrew/bin", "/usr/local/bin" });
167.     }
168. 
169.     // -------------------- Linux (best-effort) --------------------
170.     private async Task InstallLinuxAsync(IProgress<DependencyInstallProgress>? progress, CancellationToken ct)
171.     {
172.         // Try apt-get, then dnf, then pacman.
173.         var attempts = new (string exe, string args)[]
174.         {
175.             ("/usr/bin/apt-get", "install -y ffmpeg"),
176.             ("/usr/bin/dnf",     "install -y ffmpeg"),
177.             ("/usr/bin/pacman",  "-S --noconfirm ffmpeg")
178.         };
179.         foreach (var (exe, args) in attempts)
180.         {
181.             if (!File.Exists(exe)) continue;
182.             progress?.Report(new DependencyInstallProgress { Name = "ffmpeg", Stage = $"{Path.GetFileName(exe)} {args}" });
183.             var (so, se, code) = await ProcessHelper.RunAsync("sudo", $"{exe} {args}", ct,
184.                 line => progress?.Report(new DependencyInstallProgress { Name = "ffmpeg", Stage = "installing", Detail = line }));
185.             Log.Info("apt/dnf/pacman", $"exit={code}\nstdout:\n{so}\nstderr:\n{se}");
186.             if (code == 0) return;
187.         }
188.         throw new InvalidOperationException(
189.             "No supported Linux package manager succeeded. Install FFmpeg manually (sudo apt install ffmpeg / sudo dnf install ffmpeg).");
190.     }
191. 
192.     private static string? LocateExecutable(string name)
193.     {
194.         var paths = (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("PATH") ?? "")
195.             .Split(Path.PathSeparator, StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries);
196.         var candidates = OperatingSystem.IsWindows()
197.             ? new[] { name + ".exe", name + ".cmd", name }
198.             : new[] { name };
199.         foreach (var p in paths)
200.         foreach (var c in candidates)
201.         {
202.             try { var f = Path.Combine(p, c); if (File.Exists(f)) return f; }
203.             catch { }
204.         }
205.         return null;
206.     }
207. }
208. 
209. internal static class ProcessHelper
210. {
211.     public static async Task<(string stdout, string stderr, int code)> RunAsync(
212.         string exe, string args, CancellationToken ct, Action<string>? onLine = null)
213.     {
214.         var psi = new ProcessStartInfo(exe, args)
215.         {
216.             RedirectStandardOutput = true,
217.             RedirectStandardError = true,
218.             UseShellExecute = false,
219.             CreateNoWindow = true
220.         };
221.         Process? p;
222.         try { p = Process.Start(psi); }
223.         catch (Exception ex)
224.         {
225.             Log.Error("Process", $"Failed to start '{exe} {args}'", ex);
226.             throw new InvalidOperationException($"Failed to start '{exe}': {ex.Message}", ex);
227.         }
228.         if (p is null) throw new InvalidOperationException($"Failed to start '{exe}'.");
229.         using var _ = p;
230. 
231.         var soBuf = new System.Text.StringBuilder();
232.         var seBuf = new System.Text.StringBuilder();
233.         p.OutputDataReceived += (_, e) => { if (e.Data is { } l) { soBuf.AppendLine(l); onLine?.Invoke(l); } };
234.         p.ErrorDataReceived  += (_, e) => { if (e.Data is { } l) { seBuf.AppendLine(l); onLine?.Invoke(l); } };
235.         p.BeginOutputReadLine();
236.         p.BeginErrorReadLine();
237.         await p.WaitForExitAsync(ct).ConfigureAwait(false);
238.         return (soBuf.ToString(), seBuf.ToString(), p.ExitCode);
239.     }
240. }
241. 
```

465.                 Process.Start("open", dir);
466.             else

{
  "summary": "Implemented recovery for interrupted dependency installs and improved install-progress visibility.\n\n**Behavior updates**\n1. **Interrupted install is now handled as retryable**\n   - Windows winget output that indicates cancellation/termination is mapped to a clean interruption path (not a hard crash state).\n2. **Install attempts always re-check dependency state**\n   - After every install attempt (success, interruption, or failure), the app refreshes dependency detection so dashboard state is consistent and can continue.\n3. **\"Already installed / no upgrade\" is no longer treated as failure**\n   - Winget non-zero results with that message now continue as a valid outcome.\n4. **Dashboard progress bar contrast improved**\n   - Explicit high-contrast styling and larger height/min width make install progress visible during dashboard installs.\n\n**Files updated**\n- `src\\VideoEmpty.Rendering\\FFmpeg\\FFmpegDependencyManager.cs`\n- `src\\VideoEmpty.UI\\MainWindow.axaml.cs`\n- `src\\VideoEmpty.UI\\MainWindow.axaml`"
}
```

</details>

<details>
<summary>14 lines</summary>

```
Implemented recovery for interrupted dependency installs and improved install-progress visibility.

**Behavior updates**
1. **Interrupted install is now handled as retryable**
   - Windows winget output that indicates cancellation/termination is mapped to a clean interruption path (not a hard crash state).
2. **Install attempts always re-check dependency state**
   - After every install attempt (success, interruption, or failure), the app refreshes dependency detection so dashboard state is consistent and can continue.
3. **"Already installed / no upgrade" is no longer treated as failure**
   - Winget non-zero results with that message now continue as a valid outcome.
4. **Dashboard progress bar contrast improved**
   - Explicit high-contrast styling and larger height/min width make install progress visible during dashboard installs.

**Files updated**
- `src\VideoEmpty.Rendering\FFmpeg\FFmpegDependencyManager.cs`
- `src\VideoEmpty.UI\MainWindow.axaml.cs`
- `src\VideoEmpty.UI\MainWindow.axaml`
```

</details>

<sub>⏱️ 25m 20s</sub>

### ✓ Task Complete

Implemented recovery for interrupted dependency installs and improved install-progress visibility.

**Behavior updates**
1. **Interrupted install is now handled as retryable**
   - Windows winget output that indicates cancellation/termination is mapped to a clean interruption path (not a hard crash state).
2. **Install attempts always re-check dependency state**
   - After every install attempt (success, interruption, or failure), the app refreshes dependency detection so dashboard state is consistent and can continue.
3. **"Already installed / no upgrade" is no longer treated as failure**
   - Winget non-zero results with that message now continue as a valid outcome.
4. **Dashboard progress bar contrast improved**
   - Explicit high-contrast styling and larger height/min width make install progress visible during dashboard installs.

**Files updated**
- `src\VideoEmpty.Rendering\FFmpeg\FFmpegDependencyManager.cs`
- `src\VideoEmpty.UI\MainWindow.axaml.cs`
- `src\VideoEmpty.UI\MainWindow.axaml`

1. namespace VideoEmpty.Rendering.FFmpeg;
2. 
3. /// <summary>Locates ffmpeg/ffprobe binaries.</summary>
4. public class FFmpegBinaries
5. {
6.     public virtual string FFmpegPath { get; }
7.     public virtual string FFprobePath { get; }
8.     public virtual bool FFmpegFound { get; }
9.     public virtual bool FFprobeFound { get; }
10. 
11.     public FFmpegBinaries(string ffmpegPath, string ffprobePath, bool ffmpegFound, bool ffprobeFound)
12.     {
13.         FFmpegPath = ffmpegPath;
14.         FFprobePath = ffprobePath;
15.         FFmpegFound = ffmpegFound;
16.         FFprobeFound = ffprobeFound;
17.     }
18. 
19.     /// <summary>Discover ffmpeg/ffprobe on PATH or via VIDEOEMPTY_FFMPEG / VIDEOEMPTY_FFPROBE.</summary>
20.     public static FFmpegBinaries Discover()
21.     {
22.         var ffmpegEnv  = Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("VIDEOEMPTY_FFMPEG");
23.         var ffprobeEnv = Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("VIDEOEMPTY_FFPROBE");
24.         var ffmpegPath  = ffmpegEnv  ?? Which("ffmpeg")  ?? "ffmpeg";
25.         var ffprobePath = ffprobeEnv ?? Which("ffprobe") ?? "ffprobe";
26.         bool ffmpegFound  = ffmpegEnv  is not null ? File.Exists(ffmpegEnv)  : Which("ffmpeg")  is not null;
27.         bool ffprobeFound = ffprobeEnv is not null ? File.Exists(ffprobeEnv) : Which("ffprobe") is not null;
28.         VideoEmpty.Core.Diagnostics.Log.Info("FFmpegBinaries",
29.             $"ffmpeg='{ffmpegPath}' (found={ffmpegFound}); ffprobe='{ffprobePath}' (found={ffprobeFound})");
30.         return new FFmpegBinaries(ffmpegPath, ffprobePath, ffmpegFound, ffprobeFound);
31.     }
32. 
33.     public void EnsureFFprobe()
34.     {
35.         if (!FFprobeFound)
36.             throw new FileNotFoundException(BuildMissingMessage("ffprobe", FFprobePath));
37.     }
38. 
39.     public virtual void EnsureFFmpeg()
40.     {
41.         if (!FFmpegFound)
42.             throw new FileNotFoundException(BuildMissingMessage("ffmpeg", FFmpegPath));
43.     }
44. 
45.     public static string BuildMissingMessage(string tool, string attemptedPath) =>
46.         $"'{tool}' was not found (tried '{attemptedPath}'). " +
47.         $"Install FFmpeg and ensure '{tool}' is on PATH, or set the " +
48.         $"VIDEOEMPTY_{tool.ToUpperInvariant()} environment variable to its full path. " +
49.         "On Windows: 'winget install Gyan.FFmpeg'. On macOS: 'brew install ffmpeg'. " +
50.         "VideoEmpty can install this for you from the toolbar.";
51. 
52.     private static string? Which(string exe)
53.     {
54.         var paths = (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("PATH") ?? "")
55.             .Split(Path.PathSeparator, StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries);
56.         var candidates = OperatingSystem.IsWindows()
57.             ? new[] { exe + ".exe", exe + ".cmd", exe }
58.             : new[] { exe };
59.         foreach (var p in paths)
60.         foreach (var c in candidates)
61.         {
62.             try
63.             {
64.                 var full = Path.Combine(p, c);
65.                 if (File.Exists(full)) return full;
66.             }
67.             catch { }
68.         }
69.         return null;
70.     }
71. }
72. 
```

```
1. using System.Diagnostics;
2. using VideoEmpty.Core.Api;
3. using VideoEmpty.Core.Diagnostics;
4. 
5. namespace VideoEmpty.Rendering.FFmpeg;
6. 
7. /// <summary>
8. /// Detects ffmpeg/ffprobe and installs them via the platform package manager
9. /// (winget on Windows, Homebrew on macOS). Linux is supported as best-effort
10. /// (apt-get on Debian/Ubuntu, dnf on Fedora) but otherwise reports an actionable error.
11. /// </summary>
12. public sealed class FFmpegDependencyManager : IDependencyManager
13. {
14.     private readonly Func<FFmpegBinaries> _rediscover;
15.     private FFmpegBinaries _bin;
16. 
17.     public FFmpegDependencyManager(Func<FFmpegBinaries>? rediscover = null)
18.     {
19.         _rediscover = rediscover ?? FFmpegBinaries.Discover;
20.         _bin = _rediscover();
21.     }
22. 
23.     /// <summary>Currently-known binary locations (refreshed on Check/Install).</summary>
24.     public FFmpegBinaries Binaries => _bin;
25. 
26.     public bool HasMissing => !_bin.FFmpegFound || !_bin.FFprobeFound;
27. 
28.     public async Task<IReadOnlyList<DependencyStatus>> CheckAsync(CancellationToken ct = default)
29.     {
30.         _bin = _rediscover();
31.         var list = new List<DependencyStatus>();
32.         list.Add(await BuildStatusAsync("ffmpeg",  _bin.FFmpegPath,  _bin.FFmpegFound,  ct));
33.         list.Add(await BuildStatusAsync("ffprobe", _bin.FFprobePath, _bin.FFprobeFound, ct));
34.         return list;
35.     }
36. 
37.     private static async Task<DependencyStatus> BuildStatusAsync(string name, string path, bool found, CancellationToken ct)
38.     {
39.         var s = new DependencyStatus { Name = name, Path = path, State = found ? DependencyState.Installed : DependencyState.Missing };
40.         if (found)
41.         {
42.             try
43.             {
44.                 var (stdout, _, code) = await ProcessHelper.RunAsync(path, "-version", ct);
45.                 if (code == 0)
46.                 {
47.                     var firstLine = stdout.Split('\n').FirstOrDefault()?.Trim();
48.                     s.Version = firstLine;
49.                 }
50.             }
51.             catch (Exception ex)
52.             {
53.                 s.Message = ex.Message;
54.             }
55.         }
56.         else
57.         {
58.             s.Message = $"'{name}' not found on PATH.";
59.         }
60.         return s;
61.     }
62. 
63.     public async Task InstallMissingAsync(IProgress<DependencyInstallProgress>? progress = null, CancellationToken ct = default)
64.     {
65.         await CheckAsync(ct);
66.         if (!HasMissing) return;
67. 
68.         if (OperatingSystem.IsWindows())
69.             await InstallWindowsAsync(progress, ct);
70.         else if (OperatingSystem.IsMacOS())
71.             await InstallMacAsync(progress, ct);
72.         else if (OperatingSystem.IsLinux())
73.             await InstallLinuxAsync(progress, ct);
74.         else
75.             throw new PlatformNotSupportedException("Automatic install is not supported on this OS.");
76. 
77.         // Re-discover after install so subsequent calls see the new binaries.
78.         _bin = _rediscover();
79.         if (HasMissing)
80.             throw new InvalidOperationException(
81.                 "FFmpeg installation completed but the binaries were still not detected. " +
82.                 "You may need to restart the app so a new PATH is picked up.");
83.     }
84. 
85.     // -------------------- Windows (winget) --------------------
86.     private async Task InstallWindowsAsync(IProgress<DependencyInstallProgress>? progress, CancellationToken ct)
87.     {
88.         var winget = LocateExecutable("winget");
89.         if (winget is null)
90.             throw new InvalidOperationException(
91.                 "winget (Windows Package Manager) was not found. Install 'App Installer' from the Microsoft Store " +
92.                 "(https://aka.ms/getwinget), then retry. As an alternative, install FFmpeg manually from https://www.gyan.dev/ffmpeg/builds/ " +
93.                 "and add the bin folder to PATH, or set VIDEOEMPTY_FFMPEG / VIDEOEMPTY_FFPROBE.");
94. 
95.         progress?.Report(new DependencyInstallProgress { Name = "ffmpeg", Stage = "winget install Gyan.FFmpeg" });
96.         Log.Info("DepInstall", "Running winget install Gyan.FFmpeg");
97.         var args = "install --id=Gyan.FFmpeg -e --silent --accept-source-agreements --accept-package-agreements";
98.         var (stdout, stderr, code) = await ProcessHelper.RunAsync(winget, args, ct,
99.             line => progress?.Report(new DependencyInstallProgress { Name = "ffmpeg", Stage = "installing", Detail = line }));
100.         Log.Info("winget", $"exit={code}\nstdout:\n{stdout}\nstderr:\n{stderr}");
101.         if (code != 0 && IsInstallationInterrupted(stdout, stderr))
102.             throw new OperationCanceledException("Installation was interrupted. You can retry.");
103.         if (code != 0 && !IsAlreadyInstalledNoUpgrade(stdout, stderr))
104.             throw new InvalidOperationException($"winget failed (exit {code}): {(string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(stderr) ? stdout : stderr)}");
105.         if (code != 0)
106.             progress?.Report(new DependencyInstallProgress { Name = "ffmpeg", Stage = "already installed", Detail = "No upgrade available." });
107. 
108.         // winget alters PATH for new processes, but the current process won't see it.
109.         // Try to locate the freshly installed binaries and update PATH for this process.
110.         TryRefreshProcessPathFromRegistry();
111.         AddWindowsFfmpegHintPaths();
112.         var refreshed = FFmpegBinaries.Discover();
113.         if (!refreshed.FFmpegFound || !refreshed.FFprobeFound)
114.         {
115.             // Search common winget link locations.
116.             var hints = new[]
117.             {
118.                 Path.Combine(Environment.GetFolderPath(Environment.SpecialFolder.LocalApplicationData), "Microsoft", "WinGet", "Links"),
119.                 Path.Combine(Environment.GetFolderPath(Environment.SpecialFolder.LocalApplicationData), "Microsoft", "WindowsApps")
120.             };
121.             AddPaths(hints);
122.             AddWindowsFfmpegHintPaths();
123.         }
124.     }
125. 
126.     private static bool IsAlreadyInstalledNoUpgrade(string stdout, string stderr)
127.     {
128.         var text = (stdout + "\n" + stderr).ToLowerInvariant();
129.         bool alreadyInstalled = text.Contains("found an existing package already installed");
130.         bool noUpgrade =
131.             text.Contains("no available upgrade found") ||
132.             text.Contains("no newer package versions are available");
133.         return alreadyInstalled && noUpgrade;
134.     }
135. 
136.     private static bool IsInstallationInterrupted(string stdout, string stderr)
137.     {
138.         var text = (stdout + "\n" + stderr).ToLowerInvariant();
139.         return text.Contains("cancelled") ||
140.                text.Contains("canceled") ||
141.                text.Contains("terminated") ||
142.                text.Contains("operation canceled") ||
143.                text.Contains("operation cancelled");
144.     }
145. 
146.     private static void AddWindowsFfmpegHintPaths()
147.     {
148.         if (!OperatingSystem.IsWindows()) return;
149. 
150.         var hints = new List<string>();
151.         var localAppData = Environment.GetFolderPath(Environment.SpecialFolder.LocalApplicationData);
152.         var winGetPackages = Path.Combine(localAppData, "Microsoft", "WinGet", "Packages");
153.         if (Directory.Exists(winGetPackages))
154.         {
155.             foreach (var packageDir in Directory.EnumerateDirectories(winGetPackages, "Gyan.FFmpeg*"))
156.             {
157.                 foreach (var ffmpegExe in Directory.EnumerateFiles(packageDir, "ffmpeg.exe", SearchOption.AllDirectories))
158.                 {
159.                     var binDir = Path.GetDirectoryName(ffmpegExe);
160.                     if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(binDir)) hints.Add(binDir);
161.                 }
162.             }
163.         }
164. 
165.         var programFiles = Environment.GetFolderPath(Environment.SpecialFolder.ProgramFiles);
166.         var commonBin = Path.Combine(programFiles, "ffmpeg", "bin");
167.         if (Directory.Exists(commonBin)) hints.Add(commonBin);
168. 
169.         AddPaths(hints);
170.     }
171. 
172.     private static void TryRefreshProcessPathFromRegistry()
173.     {
174.         if (!OperatingSystem.IsWindows()) return;
175.         try
176.         {
177.             var machine = Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("PATH", EnvironmentVariableTarget.Machine) ?? "";
178.             var user    = Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("PATH", EnvironmentVariableTarget.User) ?? "";
179.             var combined = string.Join(Path.PathSeparator, new[] { user, machine, Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("PATH") ?? "" }
180.                 .Where(s => !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(s)));
181.             Environment.SetEnvironmentVariable("PATH", combined);
182.             Log.Info("DepInstall", "Refreshed process PATH from registry.");
183.         }
184.         catch (Exception ex) { Log.Warn("DepInstall", "Refresh PATH failed: " + ex.Message); }
185.     }
186. 
187.     private static void AddPaths(IEnumerable<string> dirs)
188.     {
189.         var current = Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("PATH") ?? "";
190.         var parts = current.Split(Path.PathSeparator, StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries).ToList();
191.         foreach (var d in dirs)
192.             if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(d) && !parts.Contains(d, StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
193.                 parts.Add(d);
194.         Environment.SetEnvironmentVariable("PATH", string.Join(Path.PathSeparator, parts));
195.     }
196. 
197.     // -------------------- macOS (Homebrew) --------------------
198.     private async Task InstallMacAsync(IProgress<DependencyInstallProgress>? progress, CancellationToken ct)
199.     {
200.         var brew = LocateExecutable("brew") ?? "/opt/homebrew/bin/brew";
201.         if (!File.Exists(brew))
202.             brew = "/usr/local/bin/brew";
203.         if (!File.Exists(brew))
204.             throw new InvalidOperationException(
205.                 "Homebrew was not found. Install it from https://brew.sh (one line in Terminal), then retry. " +
206.                 "Alternatively, install FFmpeg manually and ensure 'ffmpeg' / 'ffprobe' are on PATH, or set " +
207.                 "VIDEOEMPTY_FFMPEG / VIDEOEMPTY_FFPROBE.");
208. 
209.         progress?.Report(new DependencyInstallProgress { Name = "ffmpeg", Stage = "brew install ffmpeg" });
210.         Log.Info("DepInstall", $"Running {brew} install ffmpeg");
211.         var (stdout, stderr, code) = await ProcessHelper.RunAsync(brew, "install ffmpeg", ct,
212.             line => progress?.Report(new DependencyInstallProgress { Name = "ffmpeg", Stage = "installing", Detail = line }));
213.         Log.Info("brew", $"exit={code}\nstdout:\n{stdout}\nstderr:\n{stderr}");
214.         if (code != 0)
215.             throw new InvalidOperationException($"brew failed (exit {code}): {(string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(stderr) ? stdout : stderr)}");
216. 
217.         // Add Homebrew bin dirs to process PATH so this run can locate ffmpeg.
218.         AddPaths(new[] { "/opt/homebrew/bin", "/usr/local/bin" });
219.     }
220. 
221.     // -------------------- Linux (best-effort) --------------------
222.     private async Task InstallLinuxAsync(IProgress<DependencyInstallProgress>? progress, CancellationToken ct)
223.     {
224.         // Try apt-get, then dnf, then pacman.
225.         var attempts = new (string exe, string args)[]
226.         {
227.             ("/usr/bin/apt-get", "install -y ffmpeg"),
228.             ("/usr/bin/dnf",     "install -y ffmpeg"),
229.             ("/usr/bin/pacman",  "-S --noconfirm ffmpeg")
230.         };
231.         foreach (var (exe, args) in attempts)
232.         {
233.             if (!File.Exists(exe)) continue;
234.             progress?.Report(new DependencyInstallProgress { Name = "ffmpeg", Stage = $"{Path.GetFileName(exe)} {args}" });
235.             var (so, se, code) = await ProcessHelper.RunAsync("sudo", $"{exe} {args}", ct,
236.                 line => progress?.Report(new DependencyInstallProgress { Name = "ffmpeg", Stage = "installing", Detail = line }));
237.             Log.Info("apt/dnf/pacman", $"exit={code}\nstdout:\n{so}\nstderr:\n{se}");
238.             if (code == 0) return;
239.         }
240.         throw new InvalidOperationException(
241.             "No supported Linux package manager succeeded. Install FFmpeg manually (sudo apt install ffmpeg / sudo dnf install ffmpeg).");
242.     }
243. 
244.     private static string? LocateExecutable(string name)
245.     {
246.         var paths = (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("PATH") ?? "")
247.             .Split(Path.PathSeparator, StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries);
248.         var candidates = OperatingSystem.IsWindows()
249.             ? new[] { name + ".exe", name + ".cmd", name }
250.             : new[] { name };
251.         foreach (var p in paths)
252.         foreach (var c in candidates)
253.         {
254.             try { var f = Path.Combine(p, c); if (File.Exists(f)) return f; }
255.             catch { }
256.         }
257.         return null;
258.     }
259. }
260. 
261. internal static class ProcessHelper
262. {
263.     public static async Task<(string stdout, string stderr, int code)> RunAsync(
264.         string exe, string args, CancellationToken ct, Action<string>? onLine = null)
265.     {
266.         var psi = new ProcessStartInfo(exe, args)
267.         {
268.             RedirectStandardOutput = true,
269.             RedirectStandardError = true,
270.             UseShellExecute = false,
271.             CreateNoWindow = true
272.         };
273.         Process? p;
274.         try { p = Process.Start(psi); }
275.         catch (Exception ex)
276.         {
277.             Log.Error("Process", $"Failed to start '{exe} {args}'", ex);
278.             throw new InvalidOperationException($"Failed to start '{exe}': {ex.Message}", ex);
279.         }
280.         if (p is null) throw new InvalidOperationException($"Failed to start '{exe}'.");
281.         using var _ = p;
282. 
283.         var soBuf = new System.Text.StringBuilder();
284.         var seBuf = new System.Text.StringBuilder();
285.         p.OutputDataReceived += (_, e) => { if (e.Data is { } l) { soBuf.AppendLine(l); onLine?.Invoke(l); } };
286.         p.ErrorDataReceived  += (_, e) => { if (e.Data is { } l) { seBuf.AppendLine(l); onLine?.Invoke(l); } };
287.         p.BeginOutputReadLine();
288.         p.BeginErrorReadLine();
289.         await p.WaitForExitAsync(ct).ConfigureAwait(false);
290.         return (soBuf.ToString(), seBuf.ToString(), p.ExitCode);
291.     }
292. }
293. 
```

<sub>⏱️ 35m 40s</sub>

### 💬 Copilot

Fixed — the app now detects FFmpeg/ffprobe across restarts even when installed via winget package directories that aren’t on PATH.

I updated `FFmpegBinaries.Discover()` to fall back to searching Windows winget package locations (`%LOCALAPPDATA%\Microsoft\WinGet\Packages\Gyan.FFmpeg*`) for both `ffmpeg.exe` and `ffprobe.exe`, so it won’t keep prompting after a successful install.

7. /// <summary>

10. /// </summary>
11. public sealed class VideoEmptyApi : IVideoEmptyApi
12. {
13.     private readonly ITemplateRenderer _renderer;
14.     private readonly IVideoProbe _probe;
15.     private readonly IFramePreview _framePreview;
16.     private readonly IVideoExporter _exporter;
17.     private readonly IDependencyManager _deps;
18. 
19.     public VideoEmptyApi(
20.         ITemplateRenderer renderer,
21.         IVideoProbe probe,
22.         IFramePreview framePreview,
23.         IVideoExporter exporter,
24.         IDependencyManager deps,
25.         Func<byte[], Project, int, byte[]>? compositor = null)
26.     {
27.         _renderer = renderer;
28.         _probe = probe;
29.         _framePreview = framePreview;
30.         _exporter = exporter;
31.         _deps = deps;
32.         _compositor = compositor;
33.     }
34. 
35.     public IDependencyManager Dependencies => _deps;
36. 
37.     public Project CreateProject(string name)
38.     {
39.         var p = new Project { Name = name };
40.         foreach (var t in BuiltInTemplates.All())
41.             p.Templates.Add(t);
42.         return p;
43.     }
44. 
45.     public Project OpenProject(string path) => ProjectJson.Load(path);
46. 
47.     public void SaveProject(Project project, string path) => ProjectJson.Save(project, path);
48. 
49.     public async Task<Project> SetVideoAsync(Project project, string videoPath, CancellationToken ct = default)
50.     {
51.         var info = await _probe.ProbeAsync(videoPath, ct).ConfigureAwait(false);
52.         project.VideoPath = videoPath;
53.         project.VideoResolution = new Size(info.Width, info.Height);
54.         project.VideoFps = info.Fps;
55.         project.VideoDurationMs = info.DurationMs;
56.         return project;
57.     }
58. 
59.     public IReadOnlyList<Template> ListTemplates(Project project) => project.Templates;
60.

62.         project.Templates.FirstOrDefault(t => t.Id == templateId)
63.         ?? throw new KeyNotFoundException($"Template '{templateId}' not found.");
64. 
65.     public Template CreateTemplate(Project project, Template template)
66.     {
67.         if (project.Templates.Any(t => t.Id == template.Id))
68.             throw new InvalidOperationException($"Template id '{template.Id}' already exists.");
69.         project.Templates.Add(template);
70.         return template;
71.     }
72. 
73.     public Template UpdateTemplate(Project project, Template template)
74.     {
75.         var idx = project.Templates.FindIndex(t => t.Id == template.Id);
76.         if (idx < 0) throw new KeyNotFoundException($"Template '{template.Id}' not found.");
77.         project.Templates[idx] = template;
78.         return template;
79.     }
80. 
81.     public void DeleteTemplate(Project project, string templateId)
82.     {
83.         var t = GetTemplate(project, templateId);
84.         if (project.Instances.Any(i => i.TemplateId == templateId))
85.             throw new InvalidOperationException($"Cannot delete template '{templateId}': it is in use.");
86.         project.Templates.Remove(t);
87.     }
88. 
89.     public Template DuplicateTemplate(Project project, string templateId, string? newName = null)
90.     {
91.         var src = GetTemplate(project, templateId);
92.         var json = ProjectJson.Options;
93.         var clone = System.Text.Json.JsonSerializer.Deserialize<Template>(
94.             System.Text.Json.JsonSerializer.Serialize(src, json), json)!;
